===== source: SequenceMoves.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import java.util.*;
/**
 * Probe helper: local search over the minimum-mask hiding sets, one sequence at a time, with the
 * exact Lost. A move replaces the masks X_s of one sequence by another set of the same size that
 * keeps sup_NO(P, s) at most its current value, so |R| and the hiding are unchanged. Candidates
 * come from the exact program of theorem P8 on that sequence alone: the set least overlapping X_s,
 * and for every mask of X_s the best set avoiding that mask. The move with the largest drop in Lost
 * is applied; passes repeat until one changes nothing.
 */
public final class SequenceMoves {
  public static final class Result { public Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks; public int lost, moves, passes, candidates; }
  public static Result run(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> start,
                           Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, int maxPasses) {
    Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x = new TreeMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : start.entrySet()) if (!e.getValue().isEmpty()) x.put(e.getKey(), new TreeSet<>(e.getValue()));
    Map<Pattern, Long> total = new HashMap<>();
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : x.entrySet()) { Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, e.getKey(), e.getValue(), supIn); local.put(e.getKey(), d); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : d.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum); }
    Result r = new Result();
    for (int pass = 0; pass < maxPasses; pass++) {
      r.passes++;
      boolean improved = false;
      for (int s : new ArrayList<>(x.keySet())) {
        EventSequence seq = db.get(s); Set<Integer> cur = x.get(s);
        int remaining = SupportNO.of(p, seq.mask(cur), gap);
        List<long[]> weightings = new ArrayList<>();
        long[] avoidAll = new long[seq.length()]; for (int i : cur) avoidAll[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(avoidAll);
        for (int i : cur) { long[] w = new long[seq.length()]; w[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(w); }
        Set<Set<Integer>> seen = new HashSet<>(); seen.add(cur);
        int bestDelta = 0; Set<Integer> bestSet = null; Map<Pattern, Integer> bestDrop = null;
        for (long[] w : weightings) {
          FrontierDp.Curve c = FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, w);
          if (c.masks()[remaining] != cur.size()) continue;  // cannot happen for an exact solution; kept as a guard
          Set<Integer> cand = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c.positions()[remaining]) cand.add(i);
          if (!seen.add(cand)) continue;
          r.candidates++;
          Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, s, cand, supIn);
          int delta = 0;
          Set<Pattern> touched = new HashSet<>(d.keySet()); touched.addAll(local.get(s).keySet());
          for (Pattern q : touched) {
            if (!f.containsKey(q)) continue;  // sensitive pattern
            long before = total.getOrDefault(q, 0L), after = before - local.get(s).getOrDefault(q, 0) + d.getOrDefault(q, 0);
            boolean lostBefore = f.get(q) - before < sigma, lostAfter = f.get(q) - after < sigma;
            delta += (lostAfter ? 1 : 0) - (lostBefore ? 1 : 0);
          }
          if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestSet = cand; bestDrop = d; }
        }
        if (bestSet != null) {
          for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : local.get(s).entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) -q.getValue(), Long::sum);
          for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : bestDrop.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum);
          x.put(s, bestSet); local.put(s, bestDrop); r.moves++; improved = true;
        }
      }
      if (!improved) break;
    }
    int lost = 0; for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (e.getValue() - total.getOrDefault(e.getKey(), 0L) < sigma) lost++;
    r.masks = x; r.lost = lost;
    return r;
  }
  /** Drop of every watched pattern present in sequence s (keys of supIn) when the positions m are masked. */
  static Map<Pattern, Integer> dropIn(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, int s, Set<Integer> m, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Integer> d = new HashMap<>(); EventSequence after = db.get(s).mask(m);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> e : supIn.get(s).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(e.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != e.getValue()) d.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue() - now); }
    return d;
  }
}

===== source: SequenceMoves2.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*;
/**
 * Probe helper: local search over the minimum-mask hiding sets, one sequence at a time, with the
 * exact Lost. A move replaces the masks X_s of one sequence by another set of the same size that
 * keeps sup_NO(P, s) at most its current value, so |R| and the hiding are unchanged. Candidates
 * come from the exact program of theorem P8 on that sequence alone: the set least overlapping X_s,
 * and for every mask of X_s the best set avoiding that mask. The move with the largest drop in Lost
 * is applied; passes repeat until one changes nothing. Version 2 adds allocation moves when the
 * one-sequence moves are exhausted: for a masked sequence s1 and a sequence s2 carrying P with
 * remaining support above 0, let s1 keep one more occurrence and s2 one fewer, with the sets the
 * exact program gives for the new targets (s1 preferring its current masks, s2 keeping its current
 * masks and adding the positions vital for the fewest watched patterns), accepted only when the
 * number of masks is unchanged and Lost falls.
 */
public final class SequenceMoves2 {
  public static final class Result { public Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks; public int lost, moves, passes, candidates; }
  public static Result run(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> start,
                           Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, int maxPasses, boolean allocation) {
    Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x = new TreeMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : start.entrySet()) if (!e.getValue().isEmpty()) x.put(e.getKey(), new TreeSet<>(e.getValue()));
    Map<Pattern, Long> total = new HashMap<>();
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : x.entrySet()) { Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, e.getKey(), e.getValue(), supIn); local.put(e.getKey(), d); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : d.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum); }
    Result r = new Result();
    for (int pass = 0; pass < maxPasses; pass++) {
      r.passes++;
      boolean improved = false;
      for (int s : new ArrayList<>(x.keySet())) {
        EventSequence seq = db.get(s); Set<Integer> cur = x.get(s);
        int remaining = SupportNO.of(p, seq.mask(cur), gap);
        List<long[]> weightings = new ArrayList<>();
        long[] avoidAll = new long[seq.length()]; for (int i : cur) avoidAll[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(avoidAll);
        for (int i : cur) { long[] w = new long[seq.length()]; w[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(w); }
        Set<Set<Integer>> seen = new HashSet<>(); seen.add(cur);
        int bestDelta = 0; Set<Integer> bestSet = null; Map<Pattern, Integer> bestDrop = null;
        for (long[] w : weightings) {
          FrontierDp.Curve c = FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, w);
          if (c.masks()[remaining] != cur.size()) continue;  // cannot happen for an exact solution; kept as a guard
          Set<Integer> cand = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c.positions()[remaining]) cand.add(i);
          if (!seen.add(cand)) continue;
          r.candidates++;
          Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, s, cand, supIn);
          int delta = 0;
          Set<Pattern> touched = new HashSet<>(d.keySet()); touched.addAll(local.get(s).keySet());
          for (Pattern q : touched) {
            if (!f.containsKey(q)) continue;  // sensitive pattern
            long before = total.getOrDefault(q, 0L), after = before - local.get(s).getOrDefault(q, 0) + d.getOrDefault(q, 0);
            boolean lostBefore = f.get(q) - before < sigma, lostAfter = f.get(q) - after < sigma;
            delta += (lostAfter ? 1 : 0) - (lostBefore ? 1 : 0);
          }
          if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestSet = cand; bestDrop = d; }
        }
        if (bestSet != null) {
          for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : local.get(s).entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) -q.getValue(), Long::sum);
          for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : bestDrop.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum);
          x.put(s, bestSet); local.put(s, bestDrop); r.moves++; improved = true;
        }
      }
      if (!improved && allocation) {
        improved = allocationMove(db, gap, p, x, total, local, f, sigma, supIn, r);
      }
      if (!improved) break;
    }
    int lost = 0; for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (e.getValue() - total.getOrDefault(e.getKey(), 0L) < sigma) lost++;
    r.masks = x; r.lost = lost;
    return r;
  }
  public static int allocationMovesTried = 0;
  static boolean allocationMove(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x, Map<Pattern, Long> total,
                                Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local, Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, Result r) {
    int bestDelta = 0; int b1 = -1, b2 = -1; Set<Integer> n1 = null, n2 = null; Map<Pattern, Integer> d1 = null, d2 = null;
    Map<Integer, Integer> remaining = new HashMap<>();
    for (int s : supIn.keySet()) remaining.put(s, SupportNO.of(p, x.containsKey(s) ? db.get(s).mask(x.get(s)) : db.get(s), gap));
    // Curves once per sequence and role.
    Map<Integer, FrontierDp.Curve> lighter = new HashMap<>(), heavier = new HashMap<>();
    for (int s : supIn.keySet()) {
      EventSequence seq = db.get(s); Set<Integer> cur = x.getOrDefault(s, Set.of());
      long[] keep = new long[seq.length()]; for (int i = 0; i < keep.length; i++) keep[i] = cur.contains(i) ? 0 : CostCurve.SCALE;
      lighter.put(s, FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, keep));
      long[] add = new long[seq.length()];
      NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(seq, gap); int[] vitalCount = new int[seq.length()];
      for (Pattern q : supIn.get(s).keySet()) { int id = idx.add(q, false); for (int i : idx.vitalPositions(id)) vitalCount[i]++; }
      for (int i = 0; i < add.length; i++) add[i] = cur.contains(i) ? 0 : CostCurve.SCALE * (1 + vitalCount[i]);
      heavier.put(s, FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, add));
    }
    for (int s1 : new ArrayList<>(x.keySet())) {
      int r1 = remaining.get(s1); FrontierDp.Curve c1 = lighter.get(s1); if (r1 + 1 > c1.supNO()) continue;
      for (int s2 : supIn.keySet()) {
        if (s2 == s1) continue; int r2 = remaining.get(s2); if (r2 == 0) continue;
        FrontierDp.Curve c2 = heavier.get(s2);
        int before = x.get(s1).size() + x.getOrDefault(s2, Set.of()).size();
        if (c1.masks()[r1 + 1] + c2.masks()[r2 - 1] != before) continue;
        allocationMovesTried++;
        Set<Integer> a1 = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c1.positions()[r1 + 1]) a1.add(i);
        Set<Integer> a2 = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c2.positions()[r2 - 1]) a2.add(i);
        Map<Pattern, Integer> e1 = a1.isEmpty() ? new HashMap<>() : dropIn(db, gap, s1, a1, supIn), e2 = dropIn(db, gap, s2, a2, supIn);
        Map<Pattern, Integer> old1 = local.getOrDefault(s1, Map.of()), old2 = local.getOrDefault(s2, Map.of());
        Set<Pattern> touched = new HashSet<>(); touched.addAll(e1.keySet()); touched.addAll(e2.keySet()); touched.addAll(old1.keySet()); touched.addAll(old2.keySet());
        int delta = 0;
        for (Pattern q : touched) {
          if (!f.containsKey(q)) continue;
          long bef = total.getOrDefault(q, 0L), aft = bef - old1.getOrDefault(q, 0) - old2.getOrDefault(q, 0) + e1.getOrDefault(q, 0) + e2.getOrDefault(q, 0);
          delta += ((f.get(q) - aft < sigma) ? 1 : 0) - ((f.get(q) - bef < sigma) ? 1 : 0);
        }
        if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; b1 = s1; b2 = s2; n1 = a1; n2 = a2; d1 = e1; d2 = e2; }
      }
    }
    if (b1 < 0) return false;
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : local.getOrDefault(b1, Map.of()).entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) -q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : local.getOrDefault(b2, Map.of()).entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) -q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : d1.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : d2.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    if (n1.isEmpty()) { x.remove(b1); local.remove(b1); } else { x.put(b1, n1); local.put(b1, d1); }
    x.put(b2, n2); local.put(b2, d2); r.moves++;
    return true;
  }
  /** Drop of every watched pattern present in sequence s (keys of supIn) when the positions m are masked. */
  static Map<Pattern, Integer> dropIn(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, int s, Set<Integer> m, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Integer> d = new HashMap<>(); EventSequence after = db.get(s).mask(m);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> e : supIn.get(s).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(e.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != e.getValue()) d.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue() - now); }
    return d;
  }
}

===== source: SequenceMoves3.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*;
/**
 * Probe helper, version 3: the local search of SequenceMoves2 with the filters that let it run on the
 * full data. Lost falls only if some lost pattern Q is saved, so D_Q must fall, so the move must
 * change a sequence that now lowers the support of Q ("damaging sequence").
 *  - one-sequence moves: only damaging sequences are tried. No improving one-sequence move is lost.
 *  - allocation moves: s1 (which keeps one more occurrence of P) is a damaging sequence; s2 (which
 *    loses one more) is one of the TOP_K sequences carrying P whose next unit of support costs the
 *    fewest vital positions of watched patterns. Improving moves with s1 not damaging are not tried.
 * Everything else is as in SequenceMoves2: candidates from the exact program of theorem P8 on one
 * sequence, exact Lost, the best move of a pass applied, passes until nothing improves.
 */
public final class SequenceMoves3 {
  public static final int TOP_K = 30;
  public static final class Result { public Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks; public int lost, moves, passes, candidates, allocationTried; }
  public static Result run(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> start,
                           Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, int maxPasses, boolean allocation) {
    Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x = new TreeMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : start.entrySet()) if (!e.getValue().isEmpty()) x.put(e.getKey(), new TreeSet<>(e.getValue()));
    Map<Pattern, Long> total = new HashMap<>();
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : x.entrySet()) { Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, e.getKey(), e.getValue(), supIn); local.put(e.getKey(), d); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : d.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum); }
    Map<Integer, int[]> vitalCount = new HashMap<>();  // per sequence carrying P, computed on demand
    Map<Integer, FrontierDp.Curve> heavier = new HashMap<>();  // per sequence, invalidated when its masks change
    Result r = new Result();
    for (int pass = 0; pass < maxPasses; pass++) {
      r.passes++;
      boolean improved = false;
      for (int s : damaging(x, local, total, f, sigma)) {
        EventSequence seq = db.get(s); Set<Integer> cur = x.get(s);
        int remaining = SupportNO.of(p, seq.mask(cur), gap);
        List<long[]> weightings = new ArrayList<>();
        long[] avoidAll = new long[seq.length()]; for (int i : cur) avoidAll[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(avoidAll);
        for (int i : cur) { long[] w = new long[seq.length()]; w[i] = CostCurve.SCALE; weightings.add(w); }
        Set<Set<Integer>> seen = new HashSet<>(); seen.add(cur);
        int bestDelta = 0; Set<Integer> bestSet = null; Map<Pattern, Integer> bestDrop = null;
        for (long[] w : weightings) {
          FrontierDp.Curve c = FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, w);
          if (c.masks()[remaining] != cur.size()) continue;
          Set<Integer> cand = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c.positions()[remaining]) cand.add(i);
          if (!seen.add(cand)) continue;
          r.candidates++;
          Map<Pattern, Integer> d = dropIn(db, gap, s, cand, supIn);
          int delta = delta(total, f, sigma, local.get(s), Map.of(), d, Map.of());
          if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestSet = cand; bestDrop = d; }
        }
        if (bestSet != null) { apply(total, local, x, s, bestSet, bestDrop); heavier.remove(s); r.moves++; improved = true; }
      }
      if (!improved && allocation) improved = allocationMove(db, gap, p, x, total, local, f, sigma, supIn, r, vitalCount, heavier);
      if (!improved) break;
    }
    int lost = 0; for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (e.getValue() - total.getOrDefault(e.getKey(), 0L) < sigma) lost++;
    r.masks = x; r.lost = lost;
    return r;
  }
  /** Masked sequences that lower the support of a pattern that is now lost. */
  static List<Integer> damaging(Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local, Map<Pattern, Long> total, Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma) {
    List<Integer> out = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int s : x.keySet()) for (Pattern q : local.get(s).keySet()) if (f.containsKey(q) && f.get(q) - total.getOrDefault(q, 0L) < sigma) { out.add(s); break; }
    return out;
  }
  static int delta(Map<Pattern, Long> total, Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Pattern, Integer> old1, Map<Pattern, Integer> old2, Map<Pattern, Integer> new1, Map<Pattern, Integer> new2) {
    Set<Pattern> touched = new HashSet<>(); touched.addAll(old1.keySet()); touched.addAll(old2.keySet()); touched.addAll(new1.keySet()); touched.addAll(new2.keySet());
    int delta = 0;
    for (Pattern q : touched) {
      if (!f.containsKey(q)) continue;
      long bef = total.getOrDefault(q, 0L), aft = bef - old1.getOrDefault(q, 0) - old2.getOrDefault(q, 0) + new1.getOrDefault(q, 0) + new2.getOrDefault(q, 0);
      delta += ((f.get(q) - aft < sigma) ? 1 : 0) - ((f.get(q) - bef < sigma) ? 1 : 0);
    }
    return delta;
  }
  static void apply(Map<Pattern, Long> total, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x, int s, Set<Integer> set, Map<Pattern, Integer> drop) {
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : local.getOrDefault(s, Map.of()).entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) -q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> q : drop.entrySet()) total.merge(q.getKey(), (long) q.getValue(), Long::sum);
    if (set.isEmpty()) { x.remove(s); local.remove(s); } else { x.put(s, set); local.put(s, drop); }
  }
  static boolean allocationMove(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> x, Map<Pattern, Long> total,
                                Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> local, Map<Pattern, Long> f, long sigma, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, Result r,
                                Map<Integer, int[]> vitalCount, Map<Integer, FrontierDp.Curve> heavier) {
    Map<Integer, Integer> remaining = new HashMap<>();
    // s2 candidates: sequences carrying P with support left, ranked by the cost of one more unit.
    List<long[]> ranked = new ArrayList<>();
    for (int s : supIn.keySet()) {
      int rem = SupportNO.of(p, x.containsKey(s) ? db.get(s).mask(x.get(s)) : db.get(s), gap); remaining.put(s, rem);
      if (rem == 0) continue;
      FrontierDp.Curve c = heavier.computeIfAbsent(s, k -> heavierCurve(db, gap, p, k, x.getOrDefault(k, Set.of()), supIn, vitalCount));
      ranked.add(new long[] {c.weight()[rem - 1] - c.weight()[rem], s});
    }
    ranked.sort(Comparator.comparingLong((long[] v) -> v[0]).thenComparingLong(v -> v[1]));
    List<Integer> s2s = new ArrayList<>(); for (int k = 0; k < Math.min(TOP_K, ranked.size()); k++) s2s.add((int) ranked.get(k)[1]);
    int bestDelta = 0; int b1 = -1, b2 = -1; Set<Integer> n1 = null, n2 = null; Map<Pattern, Integer> d1 = null, d2 = null;
    for (int s1 : damaging(x, local, total, f, sigma)) {
      int r1 = remaining.get(s1);
      EventSequence seq1 = db.get(s1); Set<Integer> cur1 = x.get(s1);
      long[] keep = new long[seq1.length()]; for (int i = 0; i < keep.length; i++) keep[i] = cur1.contains(i) ? 0 : CostCurve.SCALE;
      FrontierDp.Curve c1 = FrontierDp.solve(p, seq1, gap, keep);
      if (r1 + 1 > c1.supNO()) continue;
      Set<Integer> a1 = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c1.positions()[r1 + 1]) a1.add(i);
      Map<Pattern, Integer> e1 = a1.isEmpty() ? new HashMap<>() : dropIn(db, gap, s1, a1, supIn);
      for (int s2 : s2s) {
        if (s2 == s1) continue; int r2 = remaining.get(s2);
        FrontierDp.Curve c2 = heavier.get(s2);
        int before = cur1.size() + x.getOrDefault(s2, Set.of()).size();
        if (c1.masks()[r1 + 1] + c2.masks()[r2 - 1] != before) continue;
        r.allocationTried++;
        Set<Integer> a2 = new TreeSet<>(); for (int i : c2.positions()[r2 - 1]) a2.add(i);
        Map<Pattern, Integer> e2 = dropIn(db, gap, s2, a2, supIn);
        int delta = delta(total, f, sigma, local.getOrDefault(s1, Map.of()), local.getOrDefault(s2, Map.of()), e1, e2);
        if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; b1 = s1; b2 = s2; n1 = a1; n2 = a2; d1 = e1; d2 = e2; }
      }
    }
    if (b1 < 0) return false;
    // Remove both old contributions first, then add both new ones (s1 and s2 are different sequences).
    apply(total, local, x, b1, n1, d1); apply(total, local, x, b2, n2, d2);
    heavier.remove(b1); heavier.remove(b2); r.moves++;
    return true;
  }
  static FrontierDp.Curve heavierCurve(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Pattern p, int s, Set<Integer> cur, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn, Map<Integer, int[]> vitalCount) {
    EventSequence seq = db.get(s);
    int[] vc = vitalCount.computeIfAbsent(s, k -> { NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(seq, gap); int[] c = new int[seq.length()]; for (Pattern q : supIn.get(k).keySet()) { int id = idx.add(q, false); for (int i : idx.vitalPositions(id)) c[i]++; } return c; });
    long[] add = new long[seq.length()]; for (int i = 0; i < add.length; i++) add[i] = cur.contains(i) ? 0 : CostCurve.SCALE * (1 + vc[i]);
    return FrontierDp.solve(p, seq, gap, add);
  }
  static Map<Pattern, Integer> dropIn(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, int s, Set<Integer> m, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Integer> d = new HashMap<>(); EventSequence after = db.get(s).mask(m);
    for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> e : supIn.get(s).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(e.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != e.getValue()) d.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue() - now); }
    return d;
  }
}

===== source: SmallExactProbe4.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.core.flow.NettreeFlow; import hidenosp.io.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.exp.SensitiveSampling; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*; import java.nio.file.*; import java.io.*;
/**
 * Probe: the exact least Lost among the minimum-mask hiding sets, on small instances cut from the
 * real data, against CURRENT (the method as it stands) and RW_VITAL (re-weighting on exact drops);
 * and a check of the min-cut lower bound (every pattern it declares doomed must be lost in every
 * minimum-mask set). Version 3 adds RW_LOCAL: re-weighting on the marginal damage at the current
 * solution instead of the vitality in the unmasked data. For a position outside the current set: 1
 * if it carries a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; for a position inside: the support of Q
 * regained by unmasking it alone. Sequences without masks keep their vital positions. Iteration 0
 * is CURRENT's weight, as for RW_VITAL; the best Lost is kept. Version 4 adds LS_CUR and LS_RW: the
 * one-sequence-at-a-time local search of SequenceMoves (exact Lost, exact per-sequence candidates
 * from theorem P8) started from CURRENT and from RW_VITAL's best solution.
 *
 * Instance: for a sensitive pattern P of the probe sample, K windows of W consecutive events cut
 * from real sequences carrying P, each window holding an occurrence of P and at most MAX_POSITIONS
 * positions on complete occurrences of P, plus K windows from sequences without P, drawn with a
 * fixed seed (a sequence shorter than W is kept whole). sigma is set so that the support of P must
 * fall by r = 2, 3 or 4; F_sigma with l_max = 4. Every set of |R|* positions among the positions on
 * complete occurrences of P is tried; no other position can lower the support of P.
 */
public class SmallExactProbe4 {
  static final int K = 5, W = 15, MAX_POSITIONS = 8, MAX_CANDIDATES = 40, ITERATIONS = 6; static final long MAX_SUBSETS = 250_000;
  public static void main(String[] a) throws IOException {
    String file = a[0]; int sigmaFull = Integer.parseInt(a[1]); GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, Integer.parseInt(a[2])); boolean repeated = a[3].equals("repeated");
    PrintWriter rows = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(a[4], true));
    String config = Paths.get(file).getFileName() + ";" + sigmaFull + ";" + a[2] + ";" + a[3];
    SequenceDatabase full = DatasetReader.read(Paths.get(file), DatasetReader.Format.SPMF).database();
    Map<Pattern, Long> fFull = Metrics.Context.of(full, gap, 4, sigmaFull).frequent();
    SensitiveSampling.Result sampling = SensitiveSampling.draw(fFull, 1, 4, 2026, repeated);
    int set = -1, instances = 0, skipped = 0, curOpt = 0, rwOpt = 0, localOpt = 0, bothOpt = 0, lbSoundFail = 0; long sumLsCur = 0, sumLsRw = 0; int lsCurOpt = 0, lsRwOpt = 0; long sumLocal = 0, sumBoth = 0, sumCur = 0, sumRw = 0, sumOpt = 0, sumWorst = 0, sumLb = 0, sumTrueDoomed = 0;
    for (SensitiveSampling.Draw d : sampling.draws()) {
      set++;
      Pattern p = d.patterns().get(0);
      Random rnd = new Random(2026L * 31 + set);
      List<Integer> with = new ArrayList<>(), without = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s = 0; s < full.size(); s++) (SupportNO.of(p, full.get(s), gap) == 0 ? without : with).add(s);
      Collections.shuffle(with, rnd); Collections.shuffle(without, rnd);
      List<EventSequence> chosen = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s : with) {
        if (chosen.size() == K) break;
        int[] ev = full.get(s).events();
        List<Integer> starts = new ArrayList<>(); for (int st = 0; st <= Math.max(0, ev.length - W); st++) starts.add(st);
        Collections.shuffle(starts, rnd);
        for (int st : starts) {
          EventSequence window = new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W)));
          int n = onOccurrences(p, window, gap).size();
          if (SupportNO.of(p, window, gap) > 0 && n <= MAX_POSITIONS) { chosen.add(window); break; }
        }
      }
      if (chosen.size() < K) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;SKIP;only %d windows of %d events carry P on at most %d positions%n", config, set, p, chosen.size(), W, MAX_POSITIONS); continue; }
      for (int k = 0; k < Math.min(K, without.size()); k++) {
        int[] ev = full.get(without.get(k)).events(); int st = ev.length > W ? rnd.nextInt(ev.length - W + 1) : 0;
        chosen.add(new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W))));
      }
      SequenceDatabase db = new SequenceDatabase(chosen);
      long supP = SupportNO.of(p, db, gap);
      for (int r = 2; r <= 4; r++) {
        int sigma = (int) (supP - r + 1);
        if (sigma < 1) continue;
        String where = String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;r=%d", config, set, p, r);
        HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, sigma, 0.5, 4);
        Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 4, sigma);
        Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
        Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
        List<Pattern> watched = new ArrayList<>(); for (Pattern q : f.keySet()) if (!closure.contains(q)) watched.add(q);
        List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, sigma, 0.5, closure);
        Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
        int N = db.size();
        // Candidates: positions of P's Nettree.
        List<int[]> cand = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) for (int i : onOccurrences(p, db.get(s), gap)) cand.add(new int[] {s, i});
        Sanitization cur;
        if (band.isEmpty()) cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).run(problem);
        else cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, sigma)).run(problem);
        int rstar = cur.maskCount();
        long subsets = binomial(cand.size(), rstar);
        if (cand.size() > MAX_CANDIDATES || subsets > MAX_SUBSETS) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;SKIP;%d candidates, %d masks, %d subsets%n", where, cand.size(), rstar, subsets); continue; }
        // Supports of watched patterns per sequence carrying P, and vital positions (for RW_VITAL and the bound).
        Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>(); Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, int[]>> vital = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue;
          NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(db.get(s), gap);
          Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); Map<Pattern, int[]> vs = new HashMap<>();
          for (Pattern q : watched) { int id = idx.add(q, false); int nu = idx.supNO(id); if (nu == 0) continue; su.put(q, nu); vs.put(q, idx.vitalPositions(id)); }
          supIn.put(s, su); vital.put(s, vs);
        }
        // RW_VITAL and RW_LOCAL (iteration 0 = the band weight of CURRENT in both).
        int[] rwBest = new int[2]; Sanitization rwVitalSolution = null;
        for (int mode = 0; mode < 2; mode++) {
          Map<Pattern, Double> factor = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) factor.put(q, 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1));
          Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> local = null;
          int best = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
          Sanitization bestSan = null;
          for (int it = 0; it < ITERATIONS; it++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!vital.containsKey(s)) continue;
              for (Pattern q : it == 0 ? band : watched) {
                double c = it == 0 ? 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1) : factor.get(q);
                double[] marg = (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) ? local.get(s).get(q) : null;
                if (marg != null) { for (int i = 0; i < marg.length; i++) if (marg[i] != 0) w[s][i] += c * marg[i]; continue; }
                if (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) continue;  // Q untouched by the masks of s: no marginal term
                int[] vp = vital.get(s).get(q); if (vp == null) continue; for (int pos : vp) w[s][pos] += c;
              }
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            if (san.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": RW |R| " + san.maskCount());
            int lost = session.evaluate(san).lost(); if (lost < best) { best = lost; bestSan = san; }
            if (it + 1 < ITERATIONS) {
              Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
              for (Pattern q : watched) { double m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L), tau = Math.max(1.0, 0.25 * m); double sg = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.exp(-(dq - m + 0.5) / tau)); factor.put(q, 1.0 / m + sg * (1 - sg) / tau); }
              if (mode == 1) local = marginal(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
            }
          }
          rwBest[mode] = best;
          if (mode == 0) rwVitalSolution = bestSan;
        }
        int rwLost = rwBest[0], rwLocal = rwBest[1];
        int curLost = session.evaluate(cur).lost();
        Map<Pattern, Long> watchedSup = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) watchedSup.put(q, f.get(q));
        int[] lsLost = new int[2]; int[] lsMoves = new int[2];
        Sanitization[] starts = {cur, rwVitalSolution};
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
          SequenceMoves.Result ls = SequenceMoves.run(db, gap, p, starts[k].masked(), watchedSup, sigma, supIn, 20);
          Sanitization lsSan = new Sanitization(db); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : ls.masks.entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) lsSan.mask(e.getKey(), i);
          int check = session.evaluate(lsSan).lost();
          if (check != ls.lost) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search counted " + ls.lost + " lost, the evaluator " + check);
          if (lsSan.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search |R| " + lsSan.maskCount());
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(toLists(ls.masks)), gap) >= sigma) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search does not hide P");
          if (ls.lost > session.evaluate(starts[k]).lost()) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search got worse");
          lsLost[k] = ls.lost; lsMoves[k] = ls.moves;
        }
        // Exhaustive: every set of rstar candidates that hides P.
        int opt = Integer.MAX_VALUE, worst = -1, hiding = 0;
        Map<Pattern, Long> leastDrop = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) leastDrop.put(q, Long.MAX_VALUE);
        int[] idxs = new int[rstar]; for (int k = 0; k < rstar; k++) idxs[k] = k;
        while (true) {
          Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks = new HashMap<>();
          for (int k : idxs) masks.computeIfAbsent(cand.get(k)[0], x -> new HashSet<>()).add(cand.get(k)[1]);
          Map<Integer, List<Integer>> ml = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masks.entrySet()) ml.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue()));
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(ml), gap) < sigma) {
            hiding++;
            Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, masks, supIn);
            int lost = 0;
            for (Pattern q : watched) { long dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L); if (f.get(q) - dq < sigma) lost++; if (dq < leastDrop.get(q)) leastDrop.put(q, dq); }
            opt = Math.min(opt, lost); worst = Math.max(worst, lost);
          }
          int k = rstar - 1; while (k >= 0 && idxs[k] == cand.size() - rstar + k) k--; if (k < 0) break; idxs[k]++; for (int j = k + 1; j < rstar; j++) idxs[j] = idxs[j - 1] + 1;
        }
        if (hiding == 0) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": no hiding set of size " + rstar);
        if (opt > curLost || opt > rwLost || opt > rwLocal || opt > lsLost[0] || opt > lsLost[1]) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": exhaustive optimum " + opt + " above a method (" + curLost + ", " + rwLost + ", " + rwLocal + ")");
        Set<Pattern> trueDoomed = new HashSet<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) if (f.get(q) - leastDrop.get(q) < sigma) trueDoomed.add(q);
        // The min-cut bound, as in LowerBoundProbe, for every watched pattern that some minimum set damages.
        Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> pVital = new HashMap<>(), pPos = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s : supIn.keySet()) { Nettree np = Nettree.build(p, db.get(s), gap); Set<Integer> vp = new HashSet<>(); for (int i : new NettreeFlow(np).vitalPositions()) vp.add(i); Set<Integer> pp = new HashSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < np.nodeCount(); v++) pp.add(np.position(v)); pVital.put(s, vp); pPos.put(s, pp); }
        int lbDoomed = 0;
        for (Pattern q : watched) {
          long m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, best = 0;
          for (int variant = 0; variant < 2 && best < m; variant++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!supIn.containsKey(s) || !supIn.get(s).containsKey(q)) continue;
              Nettree nq = Nettree.build(q, db.get(s), gap); int[] cutNodes;
              if (variant == 0) { boolean[] c = new NettreeFlow(nq).minCut(); List<Integer> l = new ArrayList<>(); for (int v = 0; v < c.length; v++) if (c[v]) l.add(v); cutNodes = l.stream().mapToInt(Integer::intValue).toArray(); }
              else { long[] cost = new long[nq.nodeCount()]; for (int v = 0; v < cost.length; v++) { int pos = nq.position(v); cost[v] = pVital.get(s).contains(pos) ? 0 : pPos.get(s).contains(pos) ? 1 : 2; } cutNodes = NettreeFlow.minWeightMinCut(nq, cost, null).nodes(); }
              for (int v : cutNodes) w[s][nq.position(v)] += 1.0;
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            long least = 0; for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : san.masked().entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) least += Math.round(w[e.getKey()][i]);
            best = Math.max(best, least);
          }
          if (best >= m) { lbDoomed++; if (!trueDoomed.contains(q)) lbSoundFail++; }
        }
        rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d%n", where, sigma, rstar, cand.size(), hiding, watched.size(), curLost, rwLost, rwLocal, lsLost[0], lsLost[1], lsMoves[0], lsMoves[1], opt, worst, lbDoomed, trueDoomed.size());
        rows.flush();
        sumLsCur += lsLost[0]; sumLsRw += lsLost[1]; if (lsLost[0] == opt) lsCurOpt++; if (lsLost[1] == opt) lsRwOpt++;
        instances++; sumCur += curLost; sumRw += rwLost; sumLocal += rwLocal; if (rwLocal == opt) localOpt++; if (Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal) == opt) bothOpt++; sumBoth += Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal); sumOpt += opt; sumWorst += worst; sumLb += lbDoomed; sumTrueDoomed += trueDoomed.size();
        if (curLost == opt) curOpt++; if (rwLost == opt) rwOpt++;
      }
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s: %d instances (%d skipped); Lost CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d, optimum %d, worst %d; at the optimum: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d; doomed: bound %d, exact %d, bound claims not doomed in truth %d%n",
      config, instances, skipped, sumCur, sumRw, sumLocal, sumBoth, sumLsCur, sumLsRw, sumOpt, sumWorst, curOpt, rwOpt, localOpt, bothOpt, lsCurOpt, lsRwOpt, sumLb, sumTrueDoomed, lbSoundFail);
    rows.close();
  }
  /** Positions carrying a node of P's Nettree that lies on a complete occurrence (root level to leaf level). */
  static TreeSet<Integer> onOccurrences(Pattern p, EventSequence seq, GapConstraint gap) {
    Nettree t = Nettree.build(p, seq, gap); int n = t.nodeCount();
    boolean[] fromRoot = new boolean[n], toLeaf = new boolean[n];
    // Nodes are numbered level by level, so one pass in each direction settles reachability.
    for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) { if (t.isSource(v)) fromRoot[v] = true; if (fromRoot[v]) for (int u : t.out(v)) fromRoot[u] = true; }
    for (int v = n - 1; v >= 0; v--) { if (t.isSink(v)) toLeaf[v] = true; if (toLeaf[v]) for (int u : t.in(v)) toLeaf[u] = true; }
    TreeSet<Integer> out = new TreeSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) if (fromRoot[v] && toLeaf[v]) out.add(t.position(v));
    return out;
  }
  /**
   * Marginal damage at the current solution, per masked sequence and watched pattern present there:
   * outside the masks, 1 on positions carrying a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; on a mask,
   * the support of Q regained by unmasking that position alone.
   */
  static Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> marginal(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> out = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence orig = db.get(e.getKey()), after = orig.mask(e.getValue());
      NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(after, gap);
      Map<Pattern, double[]> m = new HashMap<>();
      for (Pattern q : supIn.get(e.getKey()).keySet()) {
        double[] a = new double[orig.length()];
        int id = idx.add(q, false); int now = idx.supNO(id);
        for (int pos : idx.vitalPositions(id)) a[pos] = 1;
        for (int i : e.getValue()) { Set<Integer> rest = new HashSet<>(e.getValue()); rest.remove(i); a[i] = SupportNO.of(q, orig.mask(rest), gap) - now; }
        m.put(q, a);
      }
      out.put(e.getKey(), m);
    }
    return out;
  }
  static Map<Integer, List<Integer>> toLists(Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> m) { Map<Integer, List<Integer>> out = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : m.entrySet()) out.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue())); return out; }
  static long binomial(int n, int k) { long b = 1; for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) { b = b * (n - i) / (i + 1); if (b > Long.MAX_VALUE / 64) return Long.MAX_VALUE / 64; } return b; }
  static Map<Pattern, Long> drops(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Long> drop = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence after = db.get(e.getKey()).mask(e.getValue());
      for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> sq : supIn.get(e.getKey()).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(sq.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != sq.getValue()) drop.merge(sq.getKey(), (long) (sq.getValue() - now), Long::sum); }
    }
    return drop;
  }
}

===== source: SmallExactProbe5.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.core.flow.NettreeFlow; import hidenosp.io.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.exp.SensitiveSampling; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*; import java.nio.file.*; import java.io.*;
/**
 * Probe: the exact least Lost among the minimum-mask hiding sets, on small instances cut from the
 * real data, against CURRENT (the method as it stands) and RW_VITAL (re-weighting on exact drops);
 * and a check of the min-cut lower bound (every pattern it declares doomed must be lost in every
 * minimum-mask set). Version 3 adds RW_LOCAL: re-weighting on the marginal damage at the current
 * solution instead of the vitality in the unmasked data. For a position outside the current set: 1
 * if it carries a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; for a position inside: the support of Q
 * regained by unmasking it alone. Sequences without masks keep their vital positions. Iteration 0
 * is CURRENT's weight, as for RW_VITAL; the best Lost is kept. Version 4 adds LS_CUR and LS_RW: the
 * one-sequence-at-a-time local search of SequenceMoves (exact Lost, exact per-sequence candidates
 * from theorem P8) started from CURRENT and from RW_VITAL's best solution. Version 5 runs the local
 * search of SequenceMoves2, which adds allocation moves between sequences.
 *
 * Instance: for a sensitive pattern P of the probe sample, K windows of W consecutive events cut
 * from real sequences carrying P, each window holding an occurrence of P and at most MAX_POSITIONS
 * positions on complete occurrences of P, plus K windows from sequences without P, drawn with a
 * fixed seed (a sequence shorter than W is kept whole). sigma is set so that the support of P must
 * fall by r = 2, 3 or 4; F_sigma with l_max = 4. Every set of |R|* positions among the positions on
 * complete occurrences of P is tried; no other position can lower the support of P.
 */
public class SmallExactProbe5 {
  static final int K = 5, W = 15, MAX_POSITIONS = 8, MAX_CANDIDATES = 40, ITERATIONS = 6; static final long MAX_SUBSETS = 250_000;
  public static void main(String[] a) throws IOException {
    String file = a[0]; int sigmaFull = Integer.parseInt(a[1]); GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, Integer.parseInt(a[2])); boolean repeated = a[3].equals("repeated");
    PrintWriter rows = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(a[4], true));
    String config = Paths.get(file).getFileName() + ";" + sigmaFull + ";" + a[2] + ";" + a[3];
    SequenceDatabase full = DatasetReader.read(Paths.get(file), DatasetReader.Format.SPMF).database();
    Map<Pattern, Long> fFull = Metrics.Context.of(full, gap, 4, sigmaFull).frequent();
    SensitiveSampling.Result sampling = SensitiveSampling.draw(fFull, 1, 4, 2026, repeated);
    int set = -1, instances = 0, skipped = 0, curOpt = 0, rwOpt = 0, localOpt = 0, bothOpt = 0, lbSoundFail = 0; long sumLsCur = 0, sumLsRw = 0; int lsCurOpt = 0, lsRwOpt = 0; long sumLocal = 0, sumBoth = 0, sumCur = 0, sumRw = 0, sumOpt = 0, sumWorst = 0, sumLb = 0, sumTrueDoomed = 0;
    for (SensitiveSampling.Draw d : sampling.draws()) {
      set++;
      Pattern p = d.patterns().get(0);
      Random rnd = new Random(2026L * 31 + set);
      List<Integer> with = new ArrayList<>(), without = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s = 0; s < full.size(); s++) (SupportNO.of(p, full.get(s), gap) == 0 ? without : with).add(s);
      Collections.shuffle(with, rnd); Collections.shuffle(without, rnd);
      List<EventSequence> chosen = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s : with) {
        if (chosen.size() == K) break;
        int[] ev = full.get(s).events();
        List<Integer> starts = new ArrayList<>(); for (int st = 0; st <= Math.max(0, ev.length - W); st++) starts.add(st);
        Collections.shuffle(starts, rnd);
        for (int st : starts) {
          EventSequence window = new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W)));
          int n = onOccurrences(p, window, gap).size();
          if (SupportNO.of(p, window, gap) > 0 && n <= MAX_POSITIONS) { chosen.add(window); break; }
        }
      }
      if (chosen.size() < K) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;SKIP;only %d windows of %d events carry P on at most %d positions%n", config, set, p, chosen.size(), W, MAX_POSITIONS); continue; }
      for (int k = 0; k < Math.min(K, without.size()); k++) {
        int[] ev = full.get(without.get(k)).events(); int st = ev.length > W ? rnd.nextInt(ev.length - W + 1) : 0;
        chosen.add(new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W))));
      }
      SequenceDatabase db = new SequenceDatabase(chosen);
      long supP = SupportNO.of(p, db, gap);
      for (int r = 2; r <= 4; r++) {
        int sigma = (int) (supP - r + 1);
        if (sigma < 1) continue;
        String where = String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;r=%d", config, set, p, r);
        HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, sigma, 0.5, 4);
        Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 4, sigma);
        Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
        Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
        List<Pattern> watched = new ArrayList<>(); for (Pattern q : f.keySet()) if (!closure.contains(q)) watched.add(q);
        List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, sigma, 0.5, closure);
        Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
        int N = db.size();
        // Candidates: positions of P's Nettree.
        List<int[]> cand = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) for (int i : onOccurrences(p, db.get(s), gap)) cand.add(new int[] {s, i});
        Sanitization cur;
        if (band.isEmpty()) cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).run(problem);
        else cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, sigma)).run(problem);
        int rstar = cur.maskCount();
        long subsets = binomial(cand.size(), rstar);
        if (cand.size() > MAX_CANDIDATES || subsets > MAX_SUBSETS) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;SKIP;%d candidates, %d masks, %d subsets%n", where, cand.size(), rstar, subsets); continue; }
        // Supports of watched patterns per sequence carrying P, and vital positions (for RW_VITAL and the bound).
        Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>(); Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, int[]>> vital = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue;
          NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(db.get(s), gap);
          Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); Map<Pattern, int[]> vs = new HashMap<>();
          for (Pattern q : watched) { int id = idx.add(q, false); int nu = idx.supNO(id); if (nu == 0) continue; su.put(q, nu); vs.put(q, idx.vitalPositions(id)); }
          supIn.put(s, su); vital.put(s, vs);
        }
        // RW_VITAL and RW_LOCAL (iteration 0 = the band weight of CURRENT in both).
        int[] rwBest = new int[2]; Sanitization rwVitalSolution = null;
        for (int mode = 0; mode < 2; mode++) {
          Map<Pattern, Double> factor = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) factor.put(q, 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1));
          Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> local = null;
          int best = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
          Sanitization bestSan = null;
          for (int it = 0; it < ITERATIONS; it++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!vital.containsKey(s)) continue;
              for (Pattern q : it == 0 ? band : watched) {
                double c = it == 0 ? 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1) : factor.get(q);
                double[] marg = (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) ? local.get(s).get(q) : null;
                if (marg != null) { for (int i = 0; i < marg.length; i++) if (marg[i] != 0) w[s][i] += c * marg[i]; continue; }
                if (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) continue;  // Q untouched by the masks of s: no marginal term
                int[] vp = vital.get(s).get(q); if (vp == null) continue; for (int pos : vp) w[s][pos] += c;
              }
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            if (san.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": RW |R| " + san.maskCount());
            int lost = session.evaluate(san).lost(); if (lost < best) { best = lost; bestSan = san; }
            if (it + 1 < ITERATIONS) {
              Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
              for (Pattern q : watched) { double m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L), tau = Math.max(1.0, 0.25 * m); double sg = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.exp(-(dq - m + 0.5) / tau)); factor.put(q, 1.0 / m + sg * (1 - sg) / tau); }
              if (mode == 1) local = marginal(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
            }
          }
          rwBest[mode] = best;
          if (mode == 0) rwVitalSolution = bestSan;
        }
        int rwLost = rwBest[0], rwLocal = rwBest[1];
        int curLost = session.evaluate(cur).lost();
        Map<Pattern, Long> watchedSup = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) watchedSup.put(q, f.get(q));
        int[] lsLost = new int[2]; int[] lsMoves = new int[2];
        Sanitization[] starts = {cur, rwVitalSolution};
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
          SequenceMoves2.Result ls = SequenceMoves2.run(db, gap, p, starts[k].masked(), watchedSup, sigma, supIn, 40, true);
          Sanitization lsSan = new Sanitization(db); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : ls.masks.entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) lsSan.mask(e.getKey(), i);
          int check = session.evaluate(lsSan).lost();
          if (check != ls.lost) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search counted " + ls.lost + " lost, the evaluator " + check);
          if (lsSan.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search |R| " + lsSan.maskCount());
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(toLists(ls.masks)), gap) >= sigma) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search does not hide P");
          if (ls.lost > session.evaluate(starts[k]).lost()) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search got worse");
          lsLost[k] = ls.lost; lsMoves[k] = ls.moves;
        }
        // Exhaustive: every set of rstar candidates that hides P.
        int opt = Integer.MAX_VALUE, worst = -1, hiding = 0;
        Map<Pattern, Long> leastDrop = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) leastDrop.put(q, Long.MAX_VALUE);
        int[] idxs = new int[rstar]; for (int k = 0; k < rstar; k++) idxs[k] = k;
        while (true) {
          Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks = new HashMap<>();
          for (int k : idxs) masks.computeIfAbsent(cand.get(k)[0], x -> new HashSet<>()).add(cand.get(k)[1]);
          Map<Integer, List<Integer>> ml = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masks.entrySet()) ml.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue()));
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(ml), gap) < sigma) {
            hiding++;
            Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, masks, supIn);
            int lost = 0;
            for (Pattern q : watched) { long dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L); if (f.get(q) - dq < sigma) lost++; if (dq < leastDrop.get(q)) leastDrop.put(q, dq); }
            opt = Math.min(opt, lost); worst = Math.max(worst, lost);
          }
          int k = rstar - 1; while (k >= 0 && idxs[k] == cand.size() - rstar + k) k--; if (k < 0) break; idxs[k]++; for (int j = k + 1; j < rstar; j++) idxs[j] = idxs[j - 1] + 1;
        }
        if (hiding == 0) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": no hiding set of size " + rstar);
        if (opt > curLost || opt > rwLost || opt > rwLocal || opt > lsLost[0] || opt > lsLost[1]) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": exhaustive optimum " + opt + " above a method (" + curLost + ", " + rwLost + ", " + rwLocal + ")");
        Set<Pattern> trueDoomed = new HashSet<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) if (f.get(q) - leastDrop.get(q) < sigma) trueDoomed.add(q);
        // The min-cut bound, as in LowerBoundProbe, for every watched pattern that some minimum set damages.
        Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> pVital = new HashMap<>(), pPos = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s : supIn.keySet()) { Nettree np = Nettree.build(p, db.get(s), gap); Set<Integer> vp = new HashSet<>(); for (int i : new NettreeFlow(np).vitalPositions()) vp.add(i); Set<Integer> pp = new HashSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < np.nodeCount(); v++) pp.add(np.position(v)); pVital.put(s, vp); pPos.put(s, pp); }
        int lbDoomed = 0;
        for (Pattern q : watched) {
          long m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, best = 0;
          for (int variant = 0; variant < 2 && best < m; variant++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!supIn.containsKey(s) || !supIn.get(s).containsKey(q)) continue;
              Nettree nq = Nettree.build(q, db.get(s), gap); int[] cutNodes;
              if (variant == 0) { boolean[] c = new NettreeFlow(nq).minCut(); List<Integer> l = new ArrayList<>(); for (int v = 0; v < c.length; v++) if (c[v]) l.add(v); cutNodes = l.stream().mapToInt(Integer::intValue).toArray(); }
              else { long[] cost = new long[nq.nodeCount()]; for (int v = 0; v < cost.length; v++) { int pos = nq.position(v); cost[v] = pVital.get(s).contains(pos) ? 0 : pPos.get(s).contains(pos) ? 1 : 2; } cutNodes = NettreeFlow.minWeightMinCut(nq, cost, null).nodes(); }
              for (int v : cutNodes) w[s][nq.position(v)] += 1.0;
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            long least = 0; for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : san.masked().entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) least += Math.round(w[e.getKey()][i]);
            best = Math.max(best, least);
          }
          if (best >= m) { lbDoomed++; if (!trueDoomed.contains(q)) lbSoundFail++; }
        }
        rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d%n", where, sigma, rstar, cand.size(), hiding, watched.size(), curLost, rwLost, rwLocal, lsLost[0], lsLost[1], lsMoves[0], lsMoves[1], opt, worst, lbDoomed, trueDoomed.size());
        rows.flush();
        sumLsCur += lsLost[0]; sumLsRw += lsLost[1]; if (lsLost[0] == opt) lsCurOpt++; if (lsLost[1] == opt) lsRwOpt++;
        instances++; sumCur += curLost; sumRw += rwLost; sumLocal += rwLocal; if (rwLocal == opt) localOpt++; if (Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal) == opt) bothOpt++; sumBoth += Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal); sumOpt += opt; sumWorst += worst; sumLb += lbDoomed; sumTrueDoomed += trueDoomed.size();
        if (curLost == opt) curOpt++; if (rwLost == opt) rwOpt++;
      }
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s: %d instances (%d skipped); Lost CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d, optimum %d, worst %d; at the optimum: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d; doomed: bound %d, exact %d, bound claims not doomed in truth %d%n",
      config, instances, skipped, sumCur, sumRw, sumLocal, sumBoth, sumLsCur, sumLsRw, sumOpt, sumWorst, curOpt, rwOpt, localOpt, bothOpt, lsCurOpt, lsRwOpt, sumLb, sumTrueDoomed, lbSoundFail);
    rows.close();
  }
  /** Positions carrying a node of P's Nettree that lies on a complete occurrence (root level to leaf level). */
  static TreeSet<Integer> onOccurrences(Pattern p, EventSequence seq, GapConstraint gap) {
    Nettree t = Nettree.build(p, seq, gap); int n = t.nodeCount();
    boolean[] fromRoot = new boolean[n], toLeaf = new boolean[n];
    // Nodes are numbered level by level, so one pass in each direction settles reachability.
    for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) { if (t.isSource(v)) fromRoot[v] = true; if (fromRoot[v]) for (int u : t.out(v)) fromRoot[u] = true; }
    for (int v = n - 1; v >= 0; v--) { if (t.isSink(v)) toLeaf[v] = true; if (toLeaf[v]) for (int u : t.in(v)) toLeaf[u] = true; }
    TreeSet<Integer> out = new TreeSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) if (fromRoot[v] && toLeaf[v]) out.add(t.position(v));
    return out;
  }
  /**
   * Marginal damage at the current solution, per masked sequence and watched pattern present there:
   * outside the masks, 1 on positions carrying a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; on a mask,
   * the support of Q regained by unmasking that position alone.
   */
  static Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> marginal(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> out = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence orig = db.get(e.getKey()), after = orig.mask(e.getValue());
      NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(after, gap);
      Map<Pattern, double[]> m = new HashMap<>();
      for (Pattern q : supIn.get(e.getKey()).keySet()) {
        double[] a = new double[orig.length()];
        int id = idx.add(q, false); int now = idx.supNO(id);
        for (int pos : idx.vitalPositions(id)) a[pos] = 1;
        for (int i : e.getValue()) { Set<Integer> rest = new HashSet<>(e.getValue()); rest.remove(i); a[i] = SupportNO.of(q, orig.mask(rest), gap) - now; }
        m.put(q, a);
      }
      out.put(e.getKey(), m);
    }
    return out;
  }
  static Map<Integer, List<Integer>> toLists(Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> m) { Map<Integer, List<Integer>> out = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : m.entrySet()) out.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue())); return out; }
  static long binomial(int n, int k) { long b = 1; for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) { b = b * (n - i) / (i + 1); if (b > Long.MAX_VALUE / 64) return Long.MAX_VALUE / 64; } return b; }
  static Map<Pattern, Long> drops(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Long> drop = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence after = db.get(e.getKey()).mask(e.getValue());
      for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> sq : supIn.get(e.getKey()).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(sq.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != sq.getValue()) drop.merge(sq.getKey(), (long) (sq.getValue() - now), Long::sum); }
    }
    return drop;
  }
}

===== source: SmallExactProbe6.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.core.flow.NettreeFlow; import hidenosp.io.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.exp.SensitiveSampling; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*; import java.nio.file.*; import java.io.*;
/**
 * Probe: the exact least Lost among the minimum-mask hiding sets, on small instances cut from the
 * real data, against CURRENT (the method as it stands) and RW_VITAL (re-weighting on exact drops);
 * and a check of the min-cut lower bound (every pattern it declares doomed must be lost in every
 * minimum-mask set). Version 3 adds RW_LOCAL: re-weighting on the marginal damage at the current
 * solution instead of the vitality in the unmasked data. For a position outside the current set: 1
 * if it carries a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; for a position inside: the support of Q
 * regained by unmasking it alone. Sequences without masks keep their vital positions. Iteration 0
 * is CURRENT's weight, as for RW_VITAL; the best Lost is kept. Version 4 adds LS_CUR and LS_RW: the
 * one-sequence-at-a-time local search of SequenceMoves (exact Lost, exact per-sequence candidates
 * from theorem P8) started from CURRENT and from RW_VITAL's best solution. Version 5 runs the local
 * search of SequenceMoves2, which adds allocation moves between sequences. Version 6 runs the filtered
 * search of SequenceMoves3 (damaging sequences only; TOP_K cheapest receivers), to measure what the
 * filter loses against version 5 on the same instances.
 *
 * Instance: for a sensitive pattern P of the probe sample, K windows of W consecutive events cut
 * from real sequences carrying P, each window holding an occurrence of P and at most MAX_POSITIONS
 * positions on complete occurrences of P, plus K windows from sequences without P, drawn with a
 * fixed seed (a sequence shorter than W is kept whole). sigma is set so that the support of P must
 * fall by r = 2, 3 or 4; F_sigma with l_max = 4. Every set of |R|* positions among the positions on
 * complete occurrences of P is tried; no other position can lower the support of P.
 */
public class SmallExactProbe6 {
  static final int K = 5, W = 15, MAX_POSITIONS = 8, MAX_CANDIDATES = 40, ITERATIONS = 6; static final long MAX_SUBSETS = 250_000;
  public static void main(String[] a) throws IOException {
    String file = a[0]; int sigmaFull = Integer.parseInt(a[1]); GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, Integer.parseInt(a[2])); boolean repeated = a[3].equals("repeated");
    PrintWriter rows = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(a[4], true));
    String config = Paths.get(file).getFileName() + ";" + sigmaFull + ";" + a[2] + ";" + a[3];
    SequenceDatabase full = DatasetReader.read(Paths.get(file), DatasetReader.Format.SPMF).database();
    Map<Pattern, Long> fFull = Metrics.Context.of(full, gap, 4, sigmaFull).frequent();
    SensitiveSampling.Result sampling = SensitiveSampling.draw(fFull, 1, 4, 2026, repeated);
    int set = -1, instances = 0, skipped = 0, curOpt = 0, rwOpt = 0, localOpt = 0, bothOpt = 0, lbSoundFail = 0; long sumLsCur = 0, sumLsRw = 0; int lsCurOpt = 0, lsRwOpt = 0; long sumLocal = 0, sumBoth = 0, sumCur = 0, sumRw = 0, sumOpt = 0, sumWorst = 0, sumLb = 0, sumTrueDoomed = 0;
    for (SensitiveSampling.Draw d : sampling.draws()) {
      set++;
      Pattern p = d.patterns().get(0);
      Random rnd = new Random(2026L * 31 + set);
      List<Integer> with = new ArrayList<>(), without = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s = 0; s < full.size(); s++) (SupportNO.of(p, full.get(s), gap) == 0 ? without : with).add(s);
      Collections.shuffle(with, rnd); Collections.shuffle(without, rnd);
      List<EventSequence> chosen = new ArrayList<>();
      for (int s : with) {
        if (chosen.size() == K) break;
        int[] ev = full.get(s).events();
        List<Integer> starts = new ArrayList<>(); for (int st = 0; st <= Math.max(0, ev.length - W); st++) starts.add(st);
        Collections.shuffle(starts, rnd);
        for (int st : starts) {
          EventSequence window = new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W)));
          int n = onOccurrences(p, window, gap).size();
          if (SupportNO.of(p, window, gap) > 0 && n <= MAX_POSITIONS) { chosen.add(window); break; }
        }
      }
      if (chosen.size() < K) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;SKIP;only %d windows of %d events carry P on at most %d positions%n", config, set, p, chosen.size(), W, MAX_POSITIONS); continue; }
      for (int k = 0; k < Math.min(K, without.size()); k++) {
        int[] ev = full.get(without.get(k)).events(); int st = ev.length > W ? rnd.nextInt(ev.length - W + 1) : 0;
        chosen.add(new EventSequence(chosen.size(), Arrays.copyOfRange(ev, st, Math.min(ev.length, st + W))));
      }
      SequenceDatabase db = new SequenceDatabase(chosen);
      long supP = SupportNO.of(p, db, gap);
      for (int r = 2; r <= 4; r++) {
        int sigma = (int) (supP - r + 1);
        if (sigma < 1) continue;
        String where = String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;r=%d", config, set, p, r);
        HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, sigma, 0.5, 4);
        Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 4, sigma);
        Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
        Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
        List<Pattern> watched = new ArrayList<>(); for (Pattern q : f.keySet()) if (!closure.contains(q)) watched.add(q);
        List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, sigma, 0.5, closure);
        Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
        int N = db.size();
        // Candidates: positions of P's Nettree.
        List<int[]> cand = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) for (int i : onOccurrences(p, db.get(s), gap)) cand.add(new int[] {s, i});
        Sanitization cur;
        if (band.isEmpty()) cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).run(problem);
        else cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, sigma)).run(problem);
        int rstar = cur.maskCount();
        long subsets = binomial(cand.size(), rstar);
        if (cand.size() > MAX_CANDIDATES || subsets > MAX_SUBSETS) { skipped++; rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;SKIP;%d candidates, %d masks, %d subsets%n", where, cand.size(), rstar, subsets); continue; }
        // Supports of watched patterns per sequence carrying P, and vital positions (for RW_VITAL and the bound).
        Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>(); Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, int[]>> vital = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue;
          NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(db.get(s), gap);
          Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); Map<Pattern, int[]> vs = new HashMap<>();
          for (Pattern q : watched) { int id = idx.add(q, false); int nu = idx.supNO(id); if (nu == 0) continue; su.put(q, nu); vs.put(q, idx.vitalPositions(id)); }
          supIn.put(s, su); vital.put(s, vs);
        }
        // RW_VITAL and RW_LOCAL (iteration 0 = the band weight of CURRENT in both).
        int[] rwBest = new int[2]; Sanitization rwVitalSolution = null;
        for (int mode = 0; mode < 2; mode++) {
          Map<Pattern, Double> factor = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) factor.put(q, 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1));
          Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> local = null;
          int best = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
          Sanitization bestSan = null;
          for (int it = 0; it < ITERATIONS; it++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!vital.containsKey(s)) continue;
              for (Pattern q : it == 0 ? band : watched) {
                double c = it == 0 ? 1.0 / (f.get(q) - sigma + 1) : factor.get(q);
                double[] marg = (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) ? local.get(s).get(q) : null;
                if (marg != null) { for (int i = 0; i < marg.length; i++) if (marg[i] != 0) w[s][i] += c * marg[i]; continue; }
                if (mode == 1 && local != null && local.containsKey(s)) continue;  // Q untouched by the masks of s: no marginal term
                int[] vp = vital.get(s).get(q); if (vp == null) continue; for (int pos : vp) w[s][pos] += c;
              }
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            if (san.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": RW |R| " + san.maskCount());
            int lost = session.evaluate(san).lost(); if (lost < best) { best = lost; bestSan = san; }
            if (it + 1 < ITERATIONS) {
              Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
              for (Pattern q : watched) { double m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L), tau = Math.max(1.0, 0.25 * m); double sg = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.exp(-(dq - m + 0.5) / tau)); factor.put(q, 1.0 / m + sg * (1 - sg) / tau); }
              if (mode == 1) local = marginal(db, gap, san.masked(), supIn);
            }
          }
          rwBest[mode] = best;
          if (mode == 0) rwVitalSolution = bestSan;
        }
        int rwLost = rwBest[0], rwLocal = rwBest[1];
        int curLost = session.evaluate(cur).lost();
        Map<Pattern, Long> watchedSup = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) watchedSup.put(q, f.get(q));
        int[] lsLost = new int[2]; int[] lsMoves = new int[2];
        Sanitization[] starts = {cur, rwVitalSolution};
        for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
          SequenceMoves3.Result ls = SequenceMoves3.run(db, gap, p, starts[k].masked(), watchedSup, sigma, supIn, 40, true);
          Sanitization lsSan = new Sanitization(db); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : ls.masks.entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) lsSan.mask(e.getKey(), i);
          int check = session.evaluate(lsSan).lost();
          if (check != ls.lost) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search counted " + ls.lost + " lost, the evaluator " + check);
          if (lsSan.maskCount() != rstar) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search |R| " + lsSan.maskCount());
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(toLists(ls.masks)), gap) >= sigma) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search does not hide P");
          if (ls.lost > session.evaluate(starts[k]).lost()) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": local search got worse");
          lsLost[k] = ls.lost; lsMoves[k] = ls.moves;
        }
        // Exhaustive: every set of rstar candidates that hides P.
        int opt = Integer.MAX_VALUE, worst = -1, hiding = 0;
        Map<Pattern, Long> leastDrop = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) leastDrop.put(q, Long.MAX_VALUE);
        int[] idxs = new int[rstar]; for (int k = 0; k < rstar; k++) idxs[k] = k;
        while (true) {
          Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masks = new HashMap<>();
          for (int k : idxs) masks.computeIfAbsent(cand.get(k)[0], x -> new HashSet<>()).add(cand.get(k)[1]);
          Map<Integer, List<Integer>> ml = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masks.entrySet()) ml.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue()));
          if (SupportNO.of(p, db.mask(ml), gap) < sigma) {
            hiding++;
            Map<Pattern, Long> drop = drops(db, gap, masks, supIn);
            int lost = 0;
            for (Pattern q : watched) { long dq = drop.getOrDefault(q, 0L); if (f.get(q) - dq < sigma) lost++; if (dq < leastDrop.get(q)) leastDrop.put(q, dq); }
            opt = Math.min(opt, lost); worst = Math.max(worst, lost);
          }
          int k = rstar - 1; while (k >= 0 && idxs[k] == cand.size() - rstar + k) k--; if (k < 0) break; idxs[k]++; for (int j = k + 1; j < rstar; j++) idxs[j] = idxs[j - 1] + 1;
        }
        if (hiding == 0) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": no hiding set of size " + rstar);
        if (opt > curLost || opt > rwLost || opt > rwLocal || opt > lsLost[0] || opt > lsLost[1]) throw new IllegalStateException(where + ": exhaustive optimum " + opt + " above a method (" + curLost + ", " + rwLost + ", " + rwLocal + ")");
        Set<Pattern> trueDoomed = new HashSet<>(); for (Pattern q : watched) if (f.get(q) - leastDrop.get(q) < sigma) trueDoomed.add(q);
        // The min-cut bound, as in LowerBoundProbe, for every watched pattern that some minimum set damages.
        Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> pVital = new HashMap<>(), pPos = new HashMap<>();
        for (int s : supIn.keySet()) { Nettree np = Nettree.build(p, db.get(s), gap); Set<Integer> vp = new HashSet<>(); for (int i : new NettreeFlow(np).vitalPositions()) vp.add(i); Set<Integer> pp = new HashSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < np.nodeCount(); v++) pp.add(np.position(v)); pVital.put(s, vp); pPos.put(s, pp); }
        int lbDoomed = 0;
        for (Pattern q : watched) {
          long m = f.get(q) - sigma + 1, best = 0;
          for (int variant = 0; variant < 2 && best < m; variant++) {
            double[][] w = new double[N][];
            for (int s = 0; s < N; s++) {
              w[s] = new double[db.get(s).length()];
              if (!supIn.containsKey(s) || !supIn.get(s).containsKey(q)) continue;
              Nettree nq = Nettree.build(q, db.get(s), gap); int[] cutNodes;
              if (variant == 0) { boolean[] c = new NettreeFlow(nq).minCut(); List<Integer> l = new ArrayList<>(); for (int v = 0; v < c.length; v++) if (c[v]) l.add(v); cutNodes = l.stream().mapToInt(Integer::intValue).toArray(); }
              else { long[] cost = new long[nq.nodeCount()]; for (int v = 0; v < cost.length; v++) { int pos = nq.position(v); cost[v] = pVital.get(s).contains(pos) ? 0 : pPos.get(s).contains(pos) ? 1 : 2; } cutNodes = NettreeFlow.minWeightMinCut(nq, cost, null).nodes(); }
              for (int v : cutNodes) w[s][nq.position(v)] += 1.0;
            }
            Sanitization san = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.uniformWeights()).withExplicitWeights(w).run(problem);
            long least = 0; for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : san.masked().entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) least += Math.round(w[e.getKey()][i]);
            best = Math.max(best, least);
          }
          if (best >= m) { lbDoomed++; if (!trueDoomed.contains(q)) lbSoundFail++; }
        }
        rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d%n", where, sigma, rstar, cand.size(), hiding, watched.size(), curLost, rwLost, rwLocal, lsLost[0], lsLost[1], lsMoves[0], lsMoves[1], opt, worst, lbDoomed, trueDoomed.size());
        rows.flush();
        sumLsCur += lsLost[0]; sumLsRw += lsLost[1]; if (lsLost[0] == opt) lsCurOpt++; if (lsLost[1] == opt) lsRwOpt++;
        instances++; sumCur += curLost; sumRw += rwLost; sumLocal += rwLocal; if (rwLocal == opt) localOpt++; if (Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal) == opt) bothOpt++; sumBoth += Math.min(rwLost, rwLocal); sumOpt += opt; sumWorst += worst; sumLb += lbDoomed; sumTrueDoomed += trueDoomed.size();
        if (curLost == opt) curOpt++; if (rwLost == opt) rwOpt++;
      }
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s: %d instances (%d skipped); Lost CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d, optimum %d, worst %d; at the optimum: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, RW_LOCAL %d, best of the two %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d; doomed: bound %d, exact %d, bound claims not doomed in truth %d%n",
      config, instances, skipped, sumCur, sumRw, sumLocal, sumBoth, sumLsCur, sumLsRw, sumOpt, sumWorst, curOpt, rwOpt, localOpt, bothOpt, lsCurOpt, lsRwOpt, sumLb, sumTrueDoomed, lbSoundFail);
    rows.close();
  }
  /** Positions carrying a node of P's Nettree that lies on a complete occurrence (root level to leaf level). */
  static TreeSet<Integer> onOccurrences(Pattern p, EventSequence seq, GapConstraint gap) {
    Nettree t = Nettree.build(p, seq, gap); int n = t.nodeCount();
    boolean[] fromRoot = new boolean[n], toLeaf = new boolean[n];
    // Nodes are numbered level by level, so one pass in each direction settles reachability.
    for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) { if (t.isSource(v)) fromRoot[v] = true; if (fromRoot[v]) for (int u : t.out(v)) fromRoot[u] = true; }
    for (int v = n - 1; v >= 0; v--) { if (t.isSink(v)) toLeaf[v] = true; if (toLeaf[v]) for (int u : t.in(v)) toLeaf[u] = true; }
    TreeSet<Integer> out = new TreeSet<>(); for (int v = 0; v < n; v++) if (fromRoot[v] && toLeaf[v]) out.add(t.position(v));
    return out;
  }
  /**
   * Marginal damage at the current solution, per masked sequence and watched pattern present there:
   * outside the masks, 1 on positions carrying a vital node of Q in the masked sequence; on a mask,
   * the support of Q regained by unmasking that position alone.
   */
  static Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> marginal(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, double[]>> out = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence orig = db.get(e.getKey()), after = orig.mask(e.getValue());
      NettreeCutIndex idx = new NettreeCutIndex(after, gap);
      Map<Pattern, double[]> m = new HashMap<>();
      for (Pattern q : supIn.get(e.getKey()).keySet()) {
        double[] a = new double[orig.length()];
        int id = idx.add(q, false); int now = idx.supNO(id);
        for (int pos : idx.vitalPositions(id)) a[pos] = 1;
        for (int i : e.getValue()) { Set<Integer> rest = new HashSet<>(e.getValue()); rest.remove(i); a[i] = SupportNO.of(q, orig.mask(rest), gap) - now; }
        m.put(q, a);
      }
      out.put(e.getKey(), m);
    }
    return out;
  }
