  static Map<Integer, List<Integer>> toLists(Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> m) { Map<Integer, List<Integer>> out = new HashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : m.entrySet()) out.put(e.getKey(), new ArrayList<>(e.getValue())); return out; }
  static long binomial(int n, int k) { long b = 1; for (int i = 0; i < k; i++) { b = b * (n - i) / (i + 1); if (b > Long.MAX_VALUE / 64) return Long.MAX_VALUE / 64; } return b; }
  static Map<Pattern, Long> drops(SequenceDatabase db, GapConstraint gap, Map<Integer, Set<Integer>> masked, Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn) {
    Map<Pattern, Long> drop = new HashMap<>();
    for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : masked.entrySet()) {
      if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
      EventSequence after = db.get(e.getKey()).mask(e.getValue());
      for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Integer> sq : supIn.get(e.getKey()).entrySet()) { int now = SupportNO.of(sq.getKey(), after, gap); if (now != sq.getValue()) drop.merge(sq.getKey(), (long) (sq.getValue() - now), Long::sum); }
    }
    return drop;
  }
}

===== source: FullLsProbe.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.io.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.exp.SensitiveSampling; import java.util.*; import java.nio.file.*; import java.io.*;
/**
 * Probe on the full data: CURRENT (cut-hide), RW (AdaptiveCutHide of the working tree, 6 iterations)
 * and the filtered local search of SequenceMoves3 started from each, on the sets of the probe sample.
 * Times are wall-clock on the development machine: a probe, not a measurement.
 */
public class FullLsProbe {
  public static void main(String[] a) throws IOException {
    String file = a[0]; int sigma = Integer.parseInt(a[1]); GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, Integer.parseInt(a[2])); boolean repeated = a[3].equals("repeated");
    PrintWriter rows = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(a[4], true));
    String config = Paths.get(file).getFileName() + ";" + sigma + ";" + a[2] + ";" + a[3];
    SequenceDatabase db = DatasetReader.read(Paths.get(file), DatasetReader.Format.SPMF).database();
    Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 4, sigma);
    Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
    int set = 0; long[] sum = new long[4]; double[] secs = new double[4]; int[] fewerThanCur = new int[4];
    for (SensitiveSampling.Draw d : SensitiveSampling.draw(f, 1, 4, 2026, repeated).draws()) {
      Pattern p = d.patterns().get(0);
      HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, sigma, 0.5, 4);
      Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
      List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, sigma, 0.5, closure);
      double[] factor = FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, sigma);
      Map<Pattern, Long> watched = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (!closure.contains(e.getKey())) watched.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
      Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
      long t = System.nanoTime();
      Sanitization cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, factor).run(problem);
      double tCur = (System.nanoTime() - t) / 1e9;
      t = System.nanoTime();
      AdaptiveCutHide rwMethod = new AdaptiveCutHide(band, factor, watched, sigma, AdaptiveCutHide.DEFAULT_ITERATIONS, session::drops);
      Sanitization rw = rwMethod.run(problem);
      double tRw = (System.nanoTime() - t) / 1e9;
      Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>();
      t = System.nanoTime();
      for (int s = 0; s < db.size(); s++) { if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue; Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched.keySet()) { int v = SupportNO.of(q, db.get(s), gap); if (v > 0) su.put(q, v); } supIn.put(s, su); }
      double tSup = (System.nanoTime() - t) / 1e9;
      Sanitization[] starts = {cur, rw}; int[] lsLost = new int[2]; double[] tLs = new double[2]; int[] moves = new int[2];
      for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
        t = System.nanoTime();
        SequenceMoves3.Result ls = SequenceMoves3.run(db, gap, p, starts[k].masked(), watched, sigma, supIn, 50, true);
        tLs[k] = (System.nanoTime() - t) / 1e9 + tSup;
        Sanitization lsSan = new Sanitization(db); for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : ls.masks.entrySet()) for (int i : e.getValue()) lsSan.mask(e.getKey(), i);
        Metrics.Row row = session.evaluate(lsSan);
        if (row.lost() != ls.lost) throw new IllegalStateException(config + " set " + set + ": local search counted " + ls.lost + ", evaluator " + row.lost());
        if (lsSan.maskCount() != cur.maskCount()) throw new IllegalStateException(config + " set " + set + ": |R| " + lsSan.maskCount() + " != " + cur.maskCount());
        if (row.hidingFailure() != 0) throw new IllegalStateException(config + " set " + set + ": local search does not hide P");
        lsLost[k] = ls.lost; moves[k] = ls.moves;
      }
      int lc = session.evaluate(cur).lost(), lr = session.evaluate(rw).lost();
      if (lr > lc || lsLost[0] > lc || lsLost[1] > lr) throw new IllegalStateException(config + " set " + set + ": a later stage lost more");
      int[] lost = {lc, lr, lsLost[0], lsLost[1]}; double[] time = {tCur, tCur + tRw, tCur + tLs[0], tCur + tRw + tLs[1]};
      for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) { sum[k] += lost[k]; secs[k] += time[k]; if (lost[k] < lc) fewerThanCur[k]++; }
      rows.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s;%d;%s;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%d;%.2f;%.2f;%.2f;%.2f%n", config, set, p, cur.maskCount(), lc, lr, lsLost[0], lsLost[1], moves[0], moves[1], time[0], time[1], time[2], time[3]);
      rows.flush();
      set++;
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s: %d sets; Lost CURRENT %d, RW %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW %d; sets with fewer lost than CURRENT: RW %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW %d; seconds (probe): CURRENT %.1f, RW %.1f, LS from CURRENT %.1f, RW then LS %.1f%n",
      config, set, sum[0], sum[1], sum[2], sum[3], fewerThanCur[1], fewerThanCur[2], fewerThanCur[3], secs[0], secs[1], secs[2], secs[3]);
    rows.close();
  }
}

===== source: SaveableProbe.java =====
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.io.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.exp.SensitiveSampling; import java.util.*; import java.nio.file.*;
/**
 * Probe: which lost patterns of a solution can one local move save? For a lost Q, e_Q = D_Q - m_Q + 1
 * is the drop that must be undone. Masks are only removed from the sequences a move changes, so a
 * move changing sequence s lowers D_Q by at most d_Q^s, and a move changing s1 and s2 by at most
 * d_Q^{s1} + d_Q^{s2}. Q is saveable by a one-sequence move when e_Q <= max_s d_Q^s, by an
 * allocation move when e_Q <= the sum of its two largest d_Q^s. Counted on the RW solution
 * (AdaptiveCutHide of the working tree) of every set of the probe sample.
 */
public class SaveableProbe {
  public static void main(String[] a) {
    String file = a[0]; int sigma = Integer.parseInt(a[1]); GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, Integer.parseInt(a[2])); boolean repeated = a[3].equals("repeated");
    SequenceDatabase db = DatasetReader.read(Paths.get(file), DatasetReader.Format.SPMF).database();
    Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 4, sigma);
    Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
    int lostTotal = 0, bySingle = 0, byPair = 0; long excessSum = 0; List<Long> excesses = new ArrayList<>();
    for (SensitiveSampling.Draw d : SensitiveSampling.draw(f, 1, 4, 2026, repeated).draws()) {
      Pattern p = d.patterns().get(0);
      HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, sigma, 0.5, 4);
      Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
      List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, sigma, 0.5, closure);
      double[] factor = FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, sigma);
      Map<Pattern, Long> watched = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (!closure.contains(e.getKey())) watched.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
      Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
      Sanitization rw = new AdaptiveCutHide(band, factor, watched, sigma, AdaptiveCutHide.DEFAULT_ITERATIONS, session::drops).run(problem);
      Map<Pattern, Long> total = session.drops(rw);
      Map<Pattern, List<Integer>> perSeq = new HashMap<>();
      for (Map.Entry<Integer, Set<Integer>> e : rw.masked().entrySet()) {
        if (e.getValue().isEmpty()) continue;
        EventSequence s = db.get(e.getKey()), after = s.mask(e.getValue());
        for (Pattern q : watched.keySet()) {
          if (watched.get(q) - total.getOrDefault(q, 0L) >= sigma) continue;  // only lost patterns
          int before = SupportNO.of(q, s, gap); if (before == 0) continue;
          int dq = before - SupportNO.of(q, after, gap); if (dq > 0) perSeq.computeIfAbsent(q, k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(dq);
        }
      }
      for (Pattern q : watched.keySet()) {
        long dTotal = total.getOrDefault(q, 0L);
        if (watched.get(q) - dTotal >= sigma) continue;
        lostTotal++;
        long excess = dTotal - (watched.get(q) - sigma + 1) + 1;
        excessSum += excess; excesses.add(excess);
        List<Integer> ds = perSeq.getOrDefault(q, List.of()); List<Integer> sorted = new ArrayList<>(ds); sorted.sort(Collections.reverseOrder());
        long top1 = sorted.isEmpty() ? 0 : sorted.get(0), top2 = top1 + (sorted.size() > 1 ? sorted.get(1) : 0);
        if (excess <= top1) bySingle++;
        if (excess <= top2) byPair++;
      }
    }
    Collections.sort(excesses);
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s sigma=%d %s %s: lost patterns of RW %d; saveable by a one-sequence move %d, by a two-sequence move %d; excess median %s, max %s%n",
      Paths.get(file).getFileName(), sigma, gap, a[3], lostTotal, bySingle, byPair,
      excesses.isEmpty() ? "-" : excesses.get(excesses.size() / 2).toString(), excesses.isEmpty() ? "-" : excesses.get(excesses.size() - 1).toString());
  }
}

===== source: pkg/MaxCutProbe.java =====
package hidenosp;
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*;
/**
 * Probe: CURRENT, the adaptive weight (RW_VITAL) and the one-sequence local search on the Max-Cut
 * instances of MaxCutReductionTest (sigma = 1, l_max = 3, g+ unbounded), against the optimum over
 * every choice. A local optimum of one-vertex moves cuts at least half the edges, so its Lost is at
 * most C + |E|/2, where C = Lost - (monochromatic edges) is the same for every choice.
 */
public class MaxCutProbe {
  public static void main(String[] a) {
    Random random = new Random(Long.parseLong(a[0])); int graphs = Integer.parseInt(a[1]);
    int n = 0, curOpt = 0, rwOpt = 0, lsCurOpt = 0, lsRwOpt = 0, halfBoundBroken = 0; long sCur = 0, sRw = 0, sLsCur = 0, sLsRw = 0, sOpt = 0, sHalf = 0;
    for (int trial = 0; n < graphs && trial < 100000; trial++) {
      int vertices = 3 + random.nextInt(5);
      List<int[]> edges = MaxCutReductionTest.randomGraph(random, vertices, vertices);
      if (edges.size() < 2) continue;
      SequenceDatabase db = MaxCutReductionTest.build(vertices, edges, 1, MaxCutReductionTest.Fault.NONE);
      GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, GapConstraint.UNBOUNDED_MAX);
      Pattern p = MaxCutReductionTest.P;
      HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, 1, 0.5, 3);
      Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 3, 1);
      Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
      Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
      List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, 1, 0.5, closure);
      if (band.isEmpty()) continue;
      Map<Pattern, Long> watched = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (!closure.contains(e.getKey())) watched.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
      Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
      int opt = Integer.MAX_VALUE, c = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
      for (int choice = 0; choice < (1 << vertices); choice++) {
        Sanitization s = new Sanitization(db); int mono = 0;
        for (int v = 0; v < vertices; v++) s.mask(v, (choice >> v) & 1);
        for (int[] e : edges) if (((choice >> e[0]) & 1) == ((choice >> e[1]) & 1)) mono++;
        int lost = session.evaluate(s).lost(); opt = Math.min(opt, lost); c = lost - mono;
      }
      Sanitization cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, 1)).run(problem);
      AdaptiveCutHide rw = new AdaptiveCutHide(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, 1), watched, 1, 6, session::drops);
      Sanitization rws = rw.run(problem);
      Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>();
      for (int s = 0; s < db.size(); s++) { if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue; Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched.keySet()) { int v = SupportNO.of(q, db.get(s), gap); if (v > 0) su.put(q, v); } supIn.put(s, su); }
      int lc = session.evaluate(cur).lost(), lr = session.evaluate(rws).lost();
      int lsc = SequenceMoves.run(db, gap, p, cur.masked(), watched, 1, supIn, 50).lost;
      int lsr = SequenceMoves.run(db, gap, p, rws.masked(), watched, 1, supIn, 50).lost;
      double half = c + edges.size() / 2.0;
      if (lsc > half || lsr > half) halfBoundBroken++;
      n++; sCur += lc; sRw += lr; sLsCur += lsc; sLsRw += lsr; sOpt += opt; sHalf += (long) Math.floor(half);
      if (lc == opt) curOpt++; if (lr == opt) rwOpt++; if (lsc == opt) lsCurOpt++; if (lsr == opt) lsRwOpt++;
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "Max-Cut instances: %d graphs; Lost CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d, optimum %d, sum of floor(C + |E|/2) %d; at the optimum: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d; local-search results above C + |E|/2: %d%n",
      n, sCur, sRw, sLsCur, sLsRw, sOpt, sHalf, curOpt, rwOpt, lsCurOpt, lsRwOpt, halfBoundBroken);
  }
}

===== source: pkg/MaxCutProbe2.java =====
package hidenosp;
import hidenosp.core.*; import hidenosp.metrics.*; import hidenosp.hide.*; import hidenosp.index.NettreeCutIndex; import java.util.*;
/**
 * Probe: CURRENT, the adaptive weight (RW_VITAL) and the one-sequence local search on the Max-Cut
 * instances of MaxCutReductionTest (sigma = 1, l_max = 3, g+ unbounded), against the optimum over
 * every choice. A local optimum of one-vertex moves cuts at least half the edges, so its Lost is at
 * most C + |E|/2, where C = Lost - (monochromatic edges) is the same for every choice. Version 2 also
 * counts the graphs where CUT-HIDE and RW lose C + |E| (every edge monochromatic) and whether their
 * solutions mask the same item in every vertex sequence.
 */
public class MaxCutProbe2 {
  public static void main(String[] a) {
    Random random = new Random(Long.parseLong(a[0])); int graphs = Integer.parseInt(a[1]);
    int curWorst = 0, rwWorst = 0, curSame = 0, rwSame = 0; long sWorst = 0;
    int n = 0, curOpt = 0, rwOpt = 0, lsCurOpt = 0, lsRwOpt = 0, halfBoundBroken = 0; long sCur = 0, sRw = 0, sLsCur = 0, sLsRw = 0, sOpt = 0, sHalf = 0;
    for (int trial = 0; n < graphs && trial < 100000; trial++) {
      int vertices = 3 + random.nextInt(5);
      List<int[]> edges = MaxCutReductionTest.randomGraph(random, vertices, vertices);
      if (edges.size() < 2) continue;
      SequenceDatabase db = MaxCutReductionTest.build(vertices, edges, 1, MaxCutReductionTest.Fault.NONE);
      GapConstraint gap = GapConstraint.of(0, GapConstraint.UNBOUNDED_MAX);
      Pattern p = MaxCutReductionTest.P;
      HideProblem problem = new HideProblem(db, List.of(p), gap, HideProblem.Mode.HIDE_DB, 1, 0.5, 3);
      Metrics.Context context = Metrics.Context.of(db, gap, 3, 1);
      Map<Pattern, Long> f = context.frequent();
      Set<Pattern> closure = FrequentPatterns.closure(List.of(p), f.keySet());
      List<Pattern> band = FrequentPatterns.nearThreshold(f, 1, 0.5, closure);
      if (band.isEmpty()) continue;
      Map<Pattern, Long> watched = new LinkedHashMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<Pattern, Long> e : f.entrySet()) if (!closure.contains(e.getKey())) watched.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
      Metrics.Context.Session session = context.session(problem);
      int opt = Integer.MAX_VALUE, c = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
      for (int choice = 0; choice < (1 << vertices); choice++) {
        Sanitization s = new Sanitization(db); int mono = 0;
        for (int v = 0; v < vertices; v++) s.mask(v, (choice >> v) & 1);
        for (int[] e : edges) if (((choice >> e[0]) & 1) == ((choice >> e[1]) & 1)) mono++;
        int lost = session.evaluate(s).lost(); opt = Math.min(opt, lost); c = lost - mono;
      }
      Sanitization cur = new CutHide(CutHideConfig.standard()).withNearThreshold(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, 1)).run(problem);
      AdaptiveCutHide rw = new AdaptiveCutHide(band, FrequentPatterns.weightFactors(band, f, 1), watched, 1, 6, session::drops);
      Sanitization rws = rw.run(problem);
      Map<Integer, Map<Pattern, Integer>> supIn = new HashMap<>();
      for (int s = 0; s < db.size(); s++) { if (SupportNO.of(p, db.get(s), gap) == 0) continue; Map<Pattern, Integer> su = new HashMap<>(); for (Pattern q : watched.keySet()) { int v = SupportNO.of(q, db.get(s), gap); if (v > 0) su.put(q, v); } supIn.put(s, su); }
      int lc = session.evaluate(cur).lost(), lr = session.evaluate(rws).lost();
      if (lc == c + edges.size()) curWorst++; if (lr == c + edges.size()) rwWorst++; sWorst += c + edges.size();
      if (sameItem(cur, vertices)) curSame++; if (sameItem(rws, vertices)) rwSame++;
      int lsc = SequenceMoves.run(db, gap, p, cur.masked(), watched, 1, supIn, 50).lost;
      int lsr = SequenceMoves.run(db, gap, p, rws.masked(), watched, 1, supIn, 50).lost;
      double half = c + edges.size() / 2.0;
      if (lsc > half || lsr > half) halfBoundBroken++;
      n++; sCur += lc; sRw += lr; sLsCur += lsc; sLsRw += lsr; sOpt += opt; sHalf += (long) Math.floor(half);
      if (lc == opt) curOpt++; if (lr == opt) rwOpt++; if (lsc == opt) lsCurOpt++; if (lsr == opt) lsRwOpt++;
    }
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "Max-Cut instances: %d graphs; Lost CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d, optimum %d, sum of floor(C + |E|/2) %d; at the optimum: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d, LS from CURRENT %d, LS from RW_VITAL %d; local-search results above C + |E|/2: %d%n",
      n, sCur, sRw, sLsCur, sLsRw, sOpt, sHalf, curOpt, rwOpt, lsCurOpt, lsRwOpt, halfBoundBroken);
    System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "sum of C + |E| %d; graphs where Lost = C + |E|: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d; graphs where one item is masked in every vertex sequence: CURRENT %d, RW_VITAL %d (of %d)%n",
      sWorst, curWorst, rwWorst, curSame, rwSame, n);
  }
  static boolean sameItem(Sanitization s, int vertices) {
    Integer item = null;
    for (int v = 0; v < vertices; v++) { Set<Integer> m = s.maskedIn(v); if (m.size() != 1) return false; int i = m.iterator().next(); if (item == null) item = i; else if (item != i) return false; }
    return true;
  }
}

===== source: summarize_ls.py =====
"""Summarise the local-search probes from their row files only.
small: rows of SmallExactProbe4/5/6 (one row per small instance);
full:  rows of FullLsProbe (one row per full-data set)."""
import sys, glob, os, collections
mode, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
if mode == "small":
    cols = ["ds", "sigma", "g", "kind", "set", "pattern", "r", "sigma_small", "rstar", "candidates", "hiding_sets", "watched",
            "cur", "rw_vital", "rw_local", "ls_cur", "ls_rw", "moves_cur", "moves_rw", "opt", "worst", "bound_doomed", "true_doomed"]
    keys = ["cur", "rw_vital", "rw_local", "ls_cur", "ls_rw", "opt"]
else:
    cols = ["ds", "sigma", "g", "kind", "set", "pattern", "rstar", "cur", "rw", "ls_cur", "ls_rw", "moves_cur", "moves_rw",
            "t_cur", "t_rw", "t_ls_cur", "t_rw_ls"]
    keys = ["cur", "rw", "ls_cur", "ls_rw"]
rows = []
for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(out, "rows_*.csv"))):
    for line in open(f):
        parts = line.rstrip("\n").split(";")
        if "SKIP" in parts:
            continue
        if len(parts) != len(cols):
            raise SystemExit("malformed row in %s: %s" % (f, line))
        rows.append(dict(zip(cols, parts)))
by = collections.defaultdict(list)
for r in rows:
    by[(r["ds"].replace(".txt", ""), r["sigma"], r["g"], r["kind"])].append(r)
tot = collections.Counter(); atopt = collections.Counter(); n = 0
print("| data | sigma | gap | kind | sets | " + " | ".join(keys) + (" | at the optimum: " + " / ".join(keys[:-1]) if mode == "small" else " | sets with fewer lost than cur: rw / ls_cur / ls_rw") + " |")
print("|---|---:|---|---|---:|" + "---:|" * len(keys) + "---|")
for k in sorted(by, key=lambda k: (k[3], k[0])):
    rs = by[k]; n += len(rs)
    t = {c: sum(int(r[c]) for r in rs) for c in keys}
    for c in keys: tot[c] += t[c]
    if mode == "small":
        a = {c: sum(1 for r in rs if int(r[c]) == int(r["opt"])) for c in keys[:-1]}
        for c in a: atopt[c] += a[c]
        extra = " / ".join(str(a[c]) for c in keys[:-1])
    else:
        a = {c: sum(1 for r in rs if int(r[c]) < int(r["cur"])) for c in keys[1:]}
        for c in a: atopt[c] += a[c]
        extra = " / ".join(str(a[c]) for c in keys[1:])
    print("| %s | %s | [0,%s] | %s | %d | %s | %s |" % (k[0], k[1], k[2], k[3], len(rs), " | ".join(str(t[c]) for c in keys), extra))
print("| all | | | | %d | %s | %s |" % (n, " | ".join(str(tot[c]) for c in keys),
      " / ".join(str(atopt[c]) for c in (keys[:-1] if mode == "small" else keys[1:]))))
if mode == "small":
    print()
    for c in keys[:-1]:
        print("%s: %d above the optimum in all (%.1f%% of %d)" % (c, tot[c] - tot["opt"], 100.0 * (tot[c] - tot["opt"]) / tot["opt"], tot["opt"]))
    worse = sum(1 for r in rows if int(r["ls_rw"]) > int(r["rw_vital"]) or int(r["ls_cur"]) > int(r["cur"]))
    print("instances where the local search ended above its start: %d of %d (impossible by construction)" % (worse, len(rows)))
